========== ARQUIVO: mobile\app\materias.tsx ==========

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { ActivityIndicator, Alert, FlatList, StatusBar, StyleSheet, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { listarMaterias } from '../src/services/materiaService';
import { useAuth } from '../src/context/AuthContext';
import type { Materia } from '../src/types/quiz';

const materiaIcons: Record<string, string> = { matematica: 'ðŸ“', portugues: 'ðŸ“–', historia: 'ðŸ›ï¸', geografia: 'ðŸŒ', biologia: 'ðŸ§¬' };

export default function MateriasScreen() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const favoritesKey = `knowzy:favoritos:${user?.id || 'local'}`;
  const [materias, setMaterias] = useState<Materia[]>([]);
  const [favoritos, setFavoritos] = useState<Materia[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      try {
        const [data, saved] = await Promise.all([listarMaterias(), AsyncStorage.getItem(favoritesKey)]);
        setMaterias(data);
        if (saved) setFavoritos(JSON.parse(saved));
      } catch (error) { Alert.alert('Erro', 'NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel carregar as matÃ©rias.'); }
      finally { setLoading(false); }
    })();
  }, [favoritesKey]);
  const isFavorite = (id: string) => favoritos.some((item) => String(item.id) === id);
  const toggleFavorite = async (item: Materia) => {
    const id = String(item.id || item.slug || item.nome);
    const next = isFavorite(id) ? favoritos.filter((fav) => String(fav.id) !== id) : [...favoritos, { ...item, id }];
    setFavoritos(next);
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(favoritesKey, JSON.stringify(next));
  };
  const renderItem = ({ item }: { item: Materia }) => {
    const slug = String(item.slug || item.id || item.nome?.toLowerCase());
    return <View style={styles.card}>
      <TouchableOpacity style={styles.cardMain} activeOpacity={0.85} onPress={() => router.push({ pathname: '/estudo/[materia]' as any, params: { materia: slug, nome: item.nome } })}>
        <Text style={styles.icon}>{materiaIcons[slug.toLowerCase()] || 'ðŸ“˜'}</Text>
        <View style={styles.textWrap}><Text style={styles.title}>{item.nome}</Text><Text style={styles.subtitle}>{item.descricao || 'Continue sua jornada'}</Text></View>
        <Text style={styles.arrow}>â€º</Text>
      </TouchableOpacity>
      <TouchableOpacity style={styles.favoriteButton} onPress={() => toggleFavorite(item)} accessibilityLabel={isFavorite(String(item.id || item.slug || item.nome)) ? 'Remover dos favoritos' : 'Adicionar aos favoritos'}>
        <Ionicons name={isFavorite(String(item.id || item.slug || item.nome)) ? 'heart' : 'heart-outline'} size={22} color="#ff8fcb" />
      </TouchableOpacity>
    </View>;
  };
  return <View style={styles.safeArea}><StatusBar barStyle="light-content"/><View style={styles.container}>
    <View style={styles.headerRow}><TouchableOpacity onPress={() => router.back()}><Text style={styles.backButton}>â†</Text></TouchableOpacity><Text style={styles.header}>MatÃ©rias</Text></View>
    {loading ? <View style={styles.loading}><ActivityIndicator size="large" color="#8C61FF"/></View> : <FlatList data={materias} keyExtractor={(item) => String(item.id || item.nome)} renderItem={renderItem} contentContainerStyle={styles.list} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}/>}
  </View></View>;
}
const styles=StyleSheet.create({safeArea:{flex:1,backgroundColor:'#060312'},container:{flex:1,paddingHorizontal:18,paddingTop:52},headerRow:{flexDirection:'row',alignItems:'center',marginBottom:18,paddingTop:8},backButton:{color:'#fff',fontSize:30,fontWeight:'700',marginRight:12},header:{color:'#fff',fontSize:30,fontWeight:'800',lineHeight:36},list:{paddingBottom:24},card:{backgroundColor:'#0d0f1f',borderRadius:18,borderWidth:1,borderColor:'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)',padding:12,marginBottom:14,flexDirection:'row',alignItems:'center'},cardMain:{flex:1,flexDirection:'row',alignItems:'center',padding:4},icon:{fontSize:28,marginRight:14},textWrap:{flex:1},title:{color:'#fff',fontSize:18,fontWeight:'700'},subtitle:{color:'#b7c2ea',fontSize:12,marginTop:4},arrow:{color:'#8C61FF',fontSize:28,fontWeight:'700',marginLeft:8},favoriteButton:{width:42,height:42,alignItems:'center',justifyContent:'center'},loading:{flex:1,justifyContent:'center',alignItems:'center'}});


========== ARQUIVO: mobile\app\perfil.tsx ==========

import React from 'react';
import {
  ScrollView,
  StatusBar,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useAuth } from '../src/context/AuthContext';

export default function PerfilScreen() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { user } = useAuth();

  const nome = user?.nome || 'UsuÃ¡rio';
  const email = user?.email || 'usuario@email.com';
  const xp = Number(user?.xp ?? 0);
  const xpText = `${xp.toLocaleString('pt-BR')} XP`;

  return (
    <View style={styles.safeArea}>
      <StatusBar barStyle="light-content" />
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.content}>
        <View style={styles.headerRow}>
          <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => router.back()} style={styles.backButton}>
            <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={22} color="#fff" />
          </TouchableOpacity>
          <Text style={styles.title}>Perfil</Text>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.card}>
          <View style={styles.avatarWrap}>
            <Ionicons name="person" size={36} color="#5269A5" />
          </View>

          <Text style={styles.name}>{nome}</Text>
          <Text style={styles.email}>{email}</Text>

          <View style={styles.xpBox}>
            <Text style={styles.xpLabel}>Total de XP</Text>
            <Text style={styles.xpValue}>{xpText}</Text>
          </View>
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({

========== ARQUIVO: mobile\app\quiz.tsx ==========

import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { api } from '../src/api/api';
import {
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
  ScrollView,
  StatusBar,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { useLocalSearchParams, useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { buscarPerguntasPorMateria, listarMaterias } from '../src/services/materiaService';
import { useAuth } from '../src/context/AuthContext';
import type { Materia, Pergunta } from '../src/types/quiz';

const buildConquistasDoUsuario = (
  xp = 0,
  totalQuizzes = 0,
  materiasExploradas: string[] = [],
  ultimaPontuacao = 0,
  conquistas: string[] = []
) => {
  const lista = Array.from(new Set((conquistas || []).filter(Boolean)));

  if (Number(xp || 0) >= 50) lista.push('primeiro_login');
  if (Number(totalQuizzes || 0) >= 50) lista.push('cinquenta_quizzes');
  if (Number(materiasExploradas?.length || 0) >= 5) lista.push('explorar_materias');
  if (Number(totalQuizzes || 0) >= 5) lista.push('cinco_quizzes');
  if (Number(ultimaPontuacao || 0) >= 100) lista.push('licao_perfeita');

  return Array.from(new Set(lista));
};

export default function QuizScreen() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const params = useLocalSearchParams<{ materia?: string; nome?: string }>();
  const { user, setUser } = useAuth();
  const materiaIdParam = typeof params.materia === 'string' ? params.materia : null;
  const materiaNomeParam = typeof params.nome === 'string' ? params.nome : 'MatÃ©ria';

  const [materias, setMaterias] = useState<Materia[]>([]);
  const [materiaId, setMateriaId] = useState<string | null>(materiaIdParam);
  const [materiaNome, setMateriaNome] = useState<string>(materiaNomeParam);
  const [perguntas, setPerguntas] = useState<Pergunta[]>([]);
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [score, setScore] = useState(0);
  const [finished, setFinished] = useState(false);
  const [xpAtualizado, setXpAtualizado] = useState(false);
  const [respostas, setRespostas] = useState<{ perguntaId: string; resposta: string }[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const carregarMaterias = async () => {
      try {
        const data = await listarMaterias();
        setMaterias(data);
      } catch (error) {
        setMaterias([]);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };

    carregarMaterias();
  }, [materiaIdParam]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!materiaId) {
      setPerguntas([]);
      setFinished(false);
      setScore(0);
      setIndex(0);
      return;
    }

    const fetchQuiz = async () => {
      setLoading(true);
      const data = await buscarPerguntasPorMateria(materiaId);
      setPerguntas(data);
      setLoading(false);
    };

    fetchQuiz();
  }, [materiaId]);

  const perguntaAtual = perguntas[index];

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!finished || xpAtualizado || !user || !materiaId) return;
    setXpAtualizado(true);
    (async () => {
      const pendingKey = 'knowzy:quizPendentes';
      let pending: any[] = [];
      let syncFailed = false;
      try {
        const saved = await AsyncStorage.getItem(pendingKey);
        pending = saved ? JSON.parse(saved) : [];
        pending.push({ materiaId, respostas, criadoEm: new Date().toISOString() });
        await AsyncStorage.setItem(pendingKey, JSON.stringify(pending));
        for (let i = 0; i < pending.length; i++) {
          try {
            const response = await api.post('/quiz/finalizar', { materiaId: pending[i].materiaId, respostas: pending[i].respostas });
            const atualizado = response.data?.usuario;
            const serverXp = Number(response.data?.xpTotal ?? atualizado?.xp ?? 0);
            if (atualizado) await setUser({ ...user, ...atualizado, xp: Math.max(Number(user.xp || 0), serverXp) });
            pending.splice(i, 1); i--;
            await AsyncStorage.setItem(pendingKey, JSON.stringify(pending));
          } catch (syncError) { syncFailed = true; console.warn('HÃ¡ resultados aguardando sincronizaÃ§Ã£o:', syncError); break; }
        }
      } catch (error) { syncFailed = true; console.warn('NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel preparar a sincronizaÃ§Ã£o:', error); }
      if (syncFailed) {
        // SÃ³ concede XP local quando o backend nÃ£o respondeu. Ao reconectar, a fila Ã© enviada e o backend deduplica por questÃ£o.
        try {
          const key = 'knowzy:xp:questoesPontuadas';
          const savedPoints = await AsyncStorage.getItem(key);
          const pontuadas = savedPoints ? JSON.parse(savedPoints) : {};
          let ganho = 0;
          for (const item of respostas) {
            const question = perguntas.find((q) => String(q.id) === String(item.perguntaId));
            if (!question) continue;
            const selectedIndex = Number(item.resposta);
            const raw = question.respostaCorreta ?? question.resposta_correta;
            const correctIndex = typeof raw === 'number' ? raw : question.alternativas.indexOf(String(raw));
            const questionKey = `${materiaId}:${item.perguntaId}`;
            if (selectedIndex === correctIndex && !pontuadas[questionKey]) { pontuadas[questionKey] = true; ganho += 5; }
          }
          await AsyncStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(pontuadas));
          if (ganho > 0) await setUser({ ...user, xp: Number(user.xp || 0) + ganho });
        } catch (localError) { console.warn('Falha ao salvar XP local:', localError); }
      }
    })();
  }, [finished, xpAtualizado, user, materiaId, respostas, perguntas, setUser]);

  const progresso = useMemo(() => {
    if (!perguntas.length) return 0;
    return ((index + 1) / perguntas.length) * 100;
  }, [index, perguntas.length]);

  const responder = (resposta: string) => {
    if (!perguntaAtual) return;
    setSelected(resposta);
    const selectedIndex = perguntaAtual.alternativas.indexOf(resposta);
    const rawCorrect = perguntaAtual.respostaCorreta ?? (perguntaAtual as any).resposta_correta;
    const correctIndex = typeof rawCorrect === 'number' ? rawCorrect : perguntaAtual.alternativas.indexOf(String(rawCorrect));
    const correta = selectedIndex === correctIndex;
    setRespostas((current) => [...current, { perguntaId: String(perguntaAtual.id), resposta: String(selectedIndex) }]);
    if (correta) setScore((value) => value + 1);
    setTimeout(() => {
      if (index === perguntas.length - 1) { setFinished(true); return; }
      setIndex((value) => value + 1);
      setSelected(null);
    }, 500);
  };

  const restartQuiz = () => {
    setMateriaId(null);
    setMateriaNome('MatÃ©ria');
    setIndex(0);
    setSelected(null);
    setScore(0);
    setFinished(false);
    setPerguntas([]);
    setRespostas([]);
    setXpAtualizado(false);
  };

  const selecionarMateria = (item: Materia) => {
    const slug = String(item.slug || item.id || item.nome || 'matematica');
    setMateriaId(slug);
    setMateriaNome(item.nome || 'MatÃ©ria');
    setIndex(0);
    setSelected(null);
    setScore(0);
    setFinished(false);
    setRespostas([]);
    setXpAtualizado(false);
  };

  if (!materiaId) {
    return (
      <View style={styles.safeArea}>
        <StatusBar barStyle="light-content" />
        <View style={styles.selectContainer}>
          <Text style={styles.header}>Escolha uma matÃ©ria</Text>
          <Text style={styles.subtitle}>Selecione a disciplina para comeÃ§ar o quiz.</Text>

          {loading ? (
            <View style={styles.centered}>
              <ActivityIndicator size="large" color="#8C61FF" />
            </View>
          ) : (
            <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.subjectList}>
              {materias.map((item) => (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={String(item.id || item.slug || item.nome)}
                  style={styles.subjectCard}
                  onPress={() => selecionarMateria(item)}
                  activeOpacity={0.85}
                >
                  <Text style={styles.subjectEmoji}>{item.slug === 'matematica' ? 'ðŸ“' : item.slug === 'portugues' ? 'ðŸ“–' : item.slug === 'historia' ? 'ðŸ›ï¸' : item.slug === 'geografia' ? 'ðŸŒ' : 'ðŸ§¬'}</Text>
                  <View style={styles.subjectTextWrap}>
                    <Text style={styles.subjectName}>{item.nome}</Text>
                    <Text style={styles.subjectMeta}>ComeÃ§ar quiz</Text>
                  </View>
                  <Text style={styles.subjectArrow}>â€º</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              ))}
            </ScrollView>
          )}
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <View style={styles.safeArea}>
        <View style={styles.loading}>
          <ActivityIndicator size="large" color="#8C61FF" />
          <Text style={styles.loadingText}>Carregando quiz...</Text>
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  }

  if (!perguntaAtual && !loading) {
    return (
      <View style={styles.safeArea}>
        <View style={styles.empty}>
          <Text style={styles.emptyTitle}>Nenhuma pergunta encontrada</Text>
          <Text style={styles.emptyText}>Esta matÃ©ria ainda nÃ£o possui questÃµes cadastradas.</Text>
          <TouchableOpacity style={styles.primaryButton} onPress={() => router.back()}>
            <Text style={styles.primaryButtonText}>Voltar</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  }

  if (finished) {
    const percentual = Math.round((score / perguntas.length) * 100);

    return (
      <View style={styles.safeArea}>
        <StatusBar barStyle="light-content" />
        <View style={styles.container}>
          <Text style={styles.header}>Quiz concluÃ­do</Text>
          <View style={styles.resultCard}>
            <Text style={styles.resultTitle}>{materiaNome}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.resultValue}>{score}/{perguntas.length}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.resultSubtitle}>{percentual}% de acertos</Text>
          </View>

          <TouchableOpacity style={styles.primaryButton} onPress={restartQuiz}>
            <Text style={styles.primaryButtonText}>Jogar novamente</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity style={styles.secondaryButton} onPress={() => router.push('/conquistas')}>
            <Text style={styles.secondaryButtonText}>Ir para conquistas</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <View style={styles.safeArea}>
      <StatusBar barStyle="light-content" />
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}>
        <View style={styles.topRow}>
          <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => router.back()}>
            <Text style={styles.backButton}>â†</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
          <Text style={styles.counter}>Pergunta {index + 1}/{perguntas.length}</Text>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.progressTrack}>
          <View style={[styles.progressFill, { width: `${progresso}%` }]} />
        </View>

        <Text style={styles.title}>{materiaNome}</Text>
        <Text style={styles.question}>{perguntaAtual.enunciado}</Text>

        <View style={styles.options}>
          {perguntaAtual.alternativas.map((alternativa) => {
            const isSelected = selected === alternativa;
            const rawCorrect = perguntaAtual.respostaCorreta ?? (perguntaAtual as any).resposta_correta;
            const correctIndex = typeof rawCorrect === 'number' ? rawCorrect : perguntaAtual.alternativas.indexOf(String(rawCorrect));
            const isCorrect = perguntaAtual.alternativas.indexOf(alternativa) === correctIndex;

            return (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={alternativa}
                style={[
                  styles.option,
                  isSelected && !isCorrect && styles.optionWrong,
                  isCorrect && selected !== null && styles.optionCorrect,
                ]}
                onPress={() => responder(alternativa)}
                disabled={selected !== null}
              >
                <Text
                  style={[
                    styles.optionText,
                    isSelected && !isCorrect && styles.optionTextWrong,
                    isCorrect && selected !== null && styles.optionTextCorrect,
                  ]}
                >
                  {alternativa}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            );
          })}
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );
}

========== ARQUIVO: mobile\app\tutor-ia.tsx ==========

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { ActivityIndicator, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { api } from '../src/api/api';
const subjects=['PortuguÃªs','MatemÃ¡tica','HistÃ³ria','Geografia','Biologia','Geral'];
export default function TutorIAScreen(){const router=useRouter();const [subject,setSubject]=useState('Geral');const [question,setQuestion]=useState('');const [answer,setAnswer]=useState('');const [loading,setLoading]=useState(false);const ask=async()=>{if(!question.trim())return;setLoading(true);setAnswer('');try{const r=await api.post('/api/tutor',{materia:subject,pergunta:question});setAnswer(r.data.resposta);}catch(e:any){setAnswer(e?.response?.data?.erro||'NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel conectar ao Tutor IA. Confira a configuraÃ§Ã£o do backend.');}finally{setLoading(false);}};return <ScrollView style={styles.screen} contentContainerStyle={styles.content}><TouchableOpacity onPress={()=>router.back()}><Text style={styles.link}>â† Voltar</Text></TouchableOpacity><Text style={styles.title}>âœ¦ Tutor IA</Text><Text style={styles.muted}>Um apoio para entender conteÃºdos, revisar e pedir exemplos.</Text><Text style={styles.label}>MatÃ©ria</Text><View style={styles.subjects}>{subjects.map(s=><TouchableOpacity key={s} onPress={()=>setSubject(s)} style={[styles.chip,subject===s&&styles.selected]}><Text style={styles.chipText}>{s}</Text></TouchableOpacity>)}</View><Text style={styles.label}>Sua dÃºvida</Text><TextInput value={question} onChangeText={setQuestion} placeholder="Escreva sua pergunta..." placeholderTextColor="#8790b6" multiline style={styles.input}/><TouchableOpacity style={styles.button} onPress={ask} disabled={loading}><Text style={styles.buttonText}>{loading?'Enviando...':'Perguntar ao tutor'}</Text></TouchableOpacity>{loading&&<ActivityIndicator color="#a99bff" style={{marginTop:18}}/>}{!!answer&&<View style={styles.answer}><Text style={styles.answerTitle}>Resposta</Text><Text style={styles.answerText}>{answer}</Text></View>}</ScrollView>}
const styles=StyleSheet.create({screen:{flex:1,backgroundColor:'#060312'},content:{padding:22,paddingTop:55,paddingBottom:40},link:{color:'#a99bff'},title:{color:'#fff',fontSize:32,fontWeight:'800',marginTop:30},muted:{color:'#b8bfdc',lineHeight:22,marginTop:8},label:{color:'#fff',fontWeight:'700',marginTop:24,marginBottom:10},subjects:{flexDirection:'row',flexWrap:'wrap',gap:8},chip:{borderColor:'#424b7d',borderWidth:1,borderRadius:20,paddingHorizontal:12,paddingVertical:9},selected:{backgroundColor:'#51418b',borderColor:'#a99bff'},chipText:{color:'#fff'},input:{minHeight:130,backgroundColor:'#111936',borderColor:'#3d477a',borderWidth:1,borderRadius:14,color:'#fff',padding:14,textAlignVertical:'top'},button:{backgroundColor:'#5269a5',padding:15,borderRadius:12,alignItems:'center',marginTop:14},buttonText:{color:'#fff',fontWeight:'700'},answer:{backgroundColor:'#111936',borderRadius:14,padding:16,marginTop:20},answerTitle:{color:'#cfc6ff',fontWeight:'800',marginBottom:10},answerText:{color:'#e1e5f8',lineHeight:24}});


========== ARQUIVO: mobile\src\api\api.ts ==========


import axios, { AxiosError, InternalAxiosRequestConfig } from 'axios';
import Constants from 'expo-constants';
const getBaseUrl = (): string => {
  if (__DEV__) {
    const hostUri = Constants.expoConfig?.hostUri ?? 'localhost:3001';
    const host = hostUri.split(':')[0];

    return process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL || `http://${host}:3001`;
  }

  return process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL || 'https://api.seudominio.com';
};
export const api = axios.create({
  baseURL: getBaseUrl(),
  timeout: 15000,
  headers: {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    Accept: 'application/json',
  },
});

let authToken: string | null = null;

api.interceptors.request.use(
  async (config: InternalAxiosRequestConfig) => {
    if (authToken && config.headers) {
      config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${authToken}`;
    }

    return config;
  },
  (error: AxiosError) => Promise.reject(error)
);

api.interceptors.response.use(
  (response) => response,
  async (error: AxiosError) => {
    if (error.response?.status === 401) {
      authToken = null;
    }

    return Promise.reject(error);
  }
);

export const setAuthToken = async (token: string) => {
  authToken = token;
};

export const clearAuthToken = async () => {
  authToken = null;
};

export const getAuthToken = async () => authToken;

api.interceptors.response.use(
  (response) => response,
  (error: AxiosError) => {
    console.log('========= ERRO API =========');
    console.log('URL:', (error.config?.baseURL || '') + (error.config?.url || ''));
    console.log('STATUS:', error.response?.status);
    console.log('RESPOSTA:', error.response?.data);
    console.log('MÃ‰TODO:', error.config?.method);
    console.log('============================');

    return Promise.reject(error);
  }
);


========== ARQUIVO: mobile\src\components\Button.tsx ==========

import React from 'react';
import { ActivityIndicator, StyleSheet, Text, TouchableOpacity, ViewStyle } from 'react-native';

type ButtonProps = {
  title: string;
  onPress: () => void;
  loading?: boolean;
  variant?: 'primary' | 'secondary';
  style?: ViewStyle;
};

export default function Button({
  title,
  onPress,
  loading = false,
  variant = 'primary',
  style,
}: ButtonProps) {
  return (
    <TouchableOpacity
      style={[
        styles.button,
        variant === 'secondary' ? styles.secondary : styles.primary,
        style,
      ]}
      onPress={onPress}
      activeOpacity={0.8}
      disabled={loading}
    >
      {loading ? (
        <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" />
      ) : (
        <Text style={styles.text}>{title}</Text>
      )}
    </TouchableOpacity>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  button: {
    width: '100%',
    minHeight: 52,
    borderRadius: 16,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    paddingVertical: 12,
    paddingHorizontal: 16,
  },
  primary: {
    backgroundColor: '#8C61FF',
  },
  secondary: {
    backgroundColor: 'transparent',
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: '#8C61FF',
  },
  text: {
    color: '#fff',
    fontSize: 16,
    fontWeight: '700',
  },
});

export default function Input({
  label,
  value,
  onChangeText,
  placeholder,
  secureTextEntry = false,
  autoCapitalize = 'none',
  keyboardType = 'default',
  autoCorrect = false,
}: InputProps) {

        value={value}
        onChangeText={onChangeText}
        placeholder={placeholder}
        placeholderTextColor="#8ea0d8"

        keyboardType={keyboardType}
        autoCorrect={autoCorrect}

}

========== ARQUIVO: mobile\src\context\AuthContext.tsx ==========


import React, {
  createContext,
  useContext,
  useMemo,
  useState,
  useEffect,
} from 'react';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { setAuthToken, clearAuthToken } from '../api/api';

import type { Usuario } from '../types/auth';

type AuthContextType = {
  user: Usuario | null;
  isLoading: boolean;
  signIn: (user: Usuario) => Promise<void>;
  signOut: () => Promise<void>;
  setUser: (user: Usuario | null) => Promise<void>;
};

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | undefined>(undefined);

export const AuthProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({
  children,
}) => {
  const [user, setUserState] = useState<Usuario | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      try {
        const savedUser = await AsyncStorage.getItem('knowzy:user');
        const savedToken = await AsyncStorage.getItem('knowzy:token');
        if (savedUser) setUserState(JSON.parse(savedUser));
        if (savedToken) await setAuthToken(savedToken);
      } catch (error) { console.warn('NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel recuperar a sessÃ£o local.', error); }
      finally { setIsLoading(false); }
    })();
  }, []);

  const signIn = async (nextUser: Usuario) => {
    try {
      await AsyncStorage.setItem('knowzy:user', JSON.stringify(nextUser));
    } catch (error) {
      // Ignora falhas de storage em ambientes restritivos.
    }

    setUserState(nextUser);
  };

  const signOut = async () => {
    try {
      await AsyncStorage.multiRemove(['knowzy:user', 'knowzy:token']);
      await clearAuthToken();
    } catch (error) {
      // Ignora falhas de storage em ambientes restritivos.
    }

    setUserState(null);
  };

  const setUser = async (nextUser: Usuario | null) => {
    setUserState(nextUser);
    if (nextUser) await AsyncStorage.setItem('knowzy:user', JSON.stringify(nextUser));
    else await AsyncStorage.removeItem('knowzy:user');
  };

  const value = useMemo<AuthContextType>(
    () => ({
      user,
      isLoading,
      signIn,
      signOut,
      setUser,
    }),
    [user]
  );

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useAuth = () => {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider');
  }

  return context;
};

========== ARQUIVO: mobile\src\services\authService.ts ==========

import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { api, setAuthToken } from '../api/api';
import type { AuthResponse, CadastroPayload, LoginPayload } from '../types/auth';

export const loginApi = async (payload: LoginPayload): Promise<AuthResponse> => {
  try {
    const response = await api.post<AuthResponse>('/login', payload);
    if (response.data?.token) { await setAuthToken(response.data.token); await AsyncStorage.setItem('knowzy:token', response.data.token); }
    return response.data;
  } catch (error: any) {
    return {
      erro: error?.response?.data?.erro || 'NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel entrar. Tente novamente.',
    };
  }
};

export const cadastroApi = async (payload: CadastroPayload): Promise<AuthResponse> => {
  try {
    const response = await api.post<AuthResponse>('/cadastro', payload);
    if (response.data?.token) { await setAuthToken(response.data.token); await AsyncStorage.setItem('knowzy:token', response.data.token); }
    return response.data;
  } catch (error: any) {
    return {
      erro: error?.response?.data?.erro || 'NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel concluir o cadastro.',
    };
  }
};


========== ARQUIVO: mobile\src\services\materiaService.ts ==========

import { api } from '../api/api';
import { normalizeUtf8Text } from '../utils/text';
import type { Materia } from '../types/quiz';
import conteudosLocais from '../data/conteudosEstudo.json';
import quizFallback from '../data/quizFallback.json';

const materiasFallback: Materia[] = [
  { id: 'matematica', slug: 'matematica', nome: 'MatemÃ¡tica', descricao: 'QuestÃµes de nÃºmeros e lÃ³gica.' },
  { id: 'portugues', slug: 'portugues', nome: 'PortuguÃªs', descricao: 'InterpretaÃ§Ã£o e gramÃ¡tica.' },
  { id: 'historia', slug: 'historia', nome: 'HistÃ³ria', descricao: 'Temas histÃ³ricos do Brasil e do mundo.' },
  { id: 'geografia', slug: 'geografia', nome: 'Geografia', descricao: 'EspaÃ§o, populaÃ§Ã£o e mapas.' },
  { id: 'biologia', slug: 'biologia', nome: 'Biologia', descricao: 'Vida, cÃ©lulas e ecologia.' },
];

export const listarMaterias = async (): Promise<Materia[]> => {
  try {
    const response = await api.get('/api/materias');
    const lista = normalizeUtf8Text(response.data?.materias || response.data || []) as Materia[];
    return Array.isArray(lista) && lista.length > 0 ? lista : materiasFallback;
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error('Erro ao listar matÃ©rias:', error);
    return materiasFallback;
  }
};

export const buscarPerguntasPorMateria = async (materiaId: string) => {
  try {
    const response = await api.get(`/api/quiz/${materiaId}`);
    const perguntas = normalizeUtf8Text(response.data?.perguntas || []) as any[];
    return perguntas;
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.warn('API indisponÃ­vel; carregando questÃµes originais de fallback:', error);
    return ((quizFallback as Record<string, any[]>)[materiaId] || []).map((item) => ({ ...item, respostaCorreta: item.respostaCorreta ?? item.resposta_correta }));
  }
};


export type TopicoEstudo = { id: string; titulo: string; explicacao: string; exemplo: string };
export type ConteudoMateria = { id: string; nome: string; descricao: string; topicos: TopicoEstudo[] };

export const buscarConteudosMateria = async (materiaId: string): Promise<ConteudoMateria | null> => {
  try {
    const response = await api.get(`/api/materias/${materiaId}/conteudos`);
    return normalizeUtf8Text(response.data?.materia) as ConteudoMateria;
  } catch (error) {
    const local = (conteudosLocais as Record<string, any>)[materiaId];
    if (!local) return null;
    return {
      id: materiaId, nome: local.nome, descricao: local.descricao,
      topicos: local.topicos.map(([id, titulo, explicacao, exemplo]: string[]) => ({ id, titulo, explicacao, exemplo }))
    };
  }
};

};

  id: string;
  nome: string;
  slug?: string;
  descricao?: string;
};

export type Pergunta = {
  id: string;
  enunciado: string;
  alternativas: string[];
  respostaCorreta?: string | number;
  resposta_correta?: string | number;
  materiaId?: string;
};

export type QuizResponse = {
  materia: string;
  perguntas: Pergunta[];
};


========== ARQUIVO: mobile\src\utils\text.ts ==========

const MOJIBAKE_PATTERN = /Ãƒ|Ã‚|Ã¢â‚¬|Ã¢â‚¬Å“|Ã¢â‚¬â„¢|ÃƒÂ¡|ÃƒÂ©|ÃƒÂ³|ÃƒÂº|ÃƒÂª|ÃƒÂ§|ÃƒÂ£|ÃƒÂ±/i;

export const normalizeUtf8Text = (value: unknown): unknown => {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
    return value.map((item) => normalizeUtf8Text(item));
  }

  if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
    return Object.fromEntries(
      Object.entries(value).map(([key, item]) => [key, normalizeUtf8Text(item)])
    );
  }

  if (typeof value !== 'string') {
    return value;
  }

  if (!value || !MOJIBAKE_PATTERN.test(value)) {
    return value;
  }

  try {
    const latin1Bytes = Uint8Array.from(Array.from(value, (char) => char.charCodeAt(0)));
    const decoded = new TextDecoder('utf-8', { fatal: true }).decode(latin1Bytes);
    return decoded && decoded !== value ? decoded : value;
  } catch (error) {
    return value;
  }
};
